1	import type { ParsedPlan, ParsedPlanItem } from "./parser";
2	
3	export interface PlanStructuralFacts {
4	  itemCount: number;
5	  hasAcceptanceCriteria: boolean;
6	  hasGoalSection: boolean;
7	  itemsMissingDetails: number[];
8	  itemsMissingVerify: number[];
9	  itemsWithEmptyVerify: number[];
10	  itemsWithVagueTitle: number[];
11	  itemsWithStubTitle: number[];
12	}
13	
14	export interface PlanReadinessAssessment {
15	  ready: boolean;
16	  questions: string[];
17	  gaps: string[];
18	  structure: PlanStructuralFacts;
19	}
20	
21	const VAGUE_PREFIXES = [
22	  "improve ",
23	  "fix ",
24	  "update ",
25	  "make ",
26	  "enhance ",
27	  "optimize ",
28	  "refactor ",
29	  "support ",
30	];
31	
32	const STUB_TITLES = new Set(["todo", "wip", "tbd", "fixme", "???", "?", "n/a"]);
33	
34	function looksVague(title: string) {
35	  const lower = title.trim().toLowerCase();
36	  return VAGUE_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => lower.startsWith(prefix)) && lower.split(" ").length <= 3;
37	}
38	
39	function looksStub(title: string) {
40	  const lower = title.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[.:;,!]+$/, "");
41	  return STUB_TITLES.has(lower) || lower.length === 0;
42	}
43	
44	function hasAnyDetails(item: ParsedPlanItem) {
45	  return Boolean(item.details && item.details.trim().length > 0);
46	}
47	
48	function hasVerifyLine(item: ParsedPlanItem) {
49	  const details = item.details ?? "";
50	  return /(^|\n)\s*-?\s*verify:/im.test(details);
51	}
52	
53	function hasEmptyVerifyLine(item: ParsedPlanItem) {
54	  const details = item.details ?? "";
55	  const match = details.match(/(^|\n)\s*-?\s*verify:\s*(.*)$/im);
56	  return Boolean(match && match[2].trim().length === 0);
57	}
58	
59	function hasGlobalAcceptanceCriteria(plan: ParsedPlan) {
60	  const headerIndex = plan.markdown.search(/^##\s+Acceptance Criteria\s*$/im);
61	  if (headerIndex < 0) return false;
62	  const rest = plan.markdown.slice(headerIndex);
63	  return /(^|\n)\s*- /m.test(rest);
64	}
65	
66	function hasGoalSection(plan: ParsedPlan) {
67	  return /^##?\s+Goal\b/im.test(plan.markdown) || /^\*\*Goal:\*\*/im.test(plan.markdown);
68	}
69	
70	export function assessPlanStructure(plan: ParsedPlan): PlanStructuralFacts {
71	  const itemsMissingDetails: number[] = [];
72	  const itemsMissingVerify: number[] = [];
73	  const itemsWithEmptyVerify: number[] = [];
74	  const itemsWithVagueTitle: number[] = [];
75	  const itemsWithStubTitle: number[] = [];
76	
77	  plan.items.forEach((item, index) => {
78	    if (!hasAnyDetails(item)) {
79	      itemsMissingDetails.push(index);
80	    }
81	    if (!hasVerifyLine(item)) {
82	      itemsMissingVerify.push(index);
83	    } else if (hasEmptyVerifyLine(item)) {
84	      itemsWithEmptyVerify.push(index);
85	    }
86	    if (looksVague(item.title)) {
87	      itemsWithVagueTitle.push(index);
88	    }
89	    if (looksStub(item.title)) {
90	      itemsWithStubTitle.push(index);
91	    }
92	  });
93	
94	  return {
95	    itemCount: plan.items.length,
96	    hasAcceptanceCriteria: hasGlobalAcceptanceCriteria(plan),
97	    hasGoalSection: hasGoalSection(plan),
98	    itemsMissingDetails,
99	    itemsMissingVerify,
100	    itemsWithEmptyVerify,
101	    itemsWithVagueTitle,
102	    itemsWithStubTitle,
103	  };
104	}
105	
106	export function structureHasBlockingGaps(structure: PlanStructuralFacts): boolean {
107	  if (structure.itemCount === 0) return true;
108	  if (structure.itemsWithStubTitle.length > 0) return true;
109	
110	  const allItemsMissingDetails = structure.itemsMissingDetails.length === structure.itemCount;
111	  if (allItemsMissingDetails && !structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria) return true;
112	
113	  if (structure.itemsWithVagueTitle.length > 0
114	    && !structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria
115	    && structure.itemsMissingVerify.length === structure.itemCount
116	    && structure.itemsMissingDetails.length === structure.itemCount) {
117	    return true;
118	  }
119	
120	  return false;
121	}
122	
123	export function describeStructuralGaps(plan: ParsedPlan, structure: PlanStructuralFacts): string[] {
124	  const gaps: string[] = [];
125	
126	  if (structure.itemCount === 0) {
127	    gaps.push("No checklist items were found in the plan.");
128	  }
129	
130	  for (const index of structure.itemsWithStubTitle) {
131	    const item = plan.items[index];
132	    if (item) {
133	      gaps.push(`Item "${item.title || "(empty)"}" is a stub.`);
134	    }
135	  }
136	
137	  for (const index of structure.itemsWithVagueTitle) {
138	    const item = plan.items[index];
139	    if (!item) continue;
140	    if (!structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria
141	      && structure.itemsMissingVerify.includes(index)
142	      && structure.itemsMissingDetails.includes(index)) {
143	      gaps.push(`Item "${item.title}" is too vague.`);
144	    }
145	  }
146	
147	  return Array.from(new Set(gaps));
148	}
149	
150	export async function assessPlanReadiness(plan: ParsedPlan): Promise<PlanReadinessAssessment> {
151	  const structure = assessPlanStructure(plan);
152	  const gaps = describeStructuralGaps(plan, structure);
153	  const blocking = structureHasBlockingGaps(structure);
154	
155	  const questions: string[] = [];
156	  if (structure.itemCount === 0) {
157	    questions.push("Please add concrete checklist items with observable outcomes.");
158	  }
159	  for (const index of structure.itemsWithVagueTitle) {
160	    const item = plan.items[index];
161	    if (!item) continue;
162	    if (!structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria
163	      && structure.itemsMissingVerify.includes(index)
164	      && structure.itemsMissingDetails.includes(index)) {
165	      questions.push(`What concrete deliverable should satisfy "${item.title}"?`);
166	      questions.push(`How will we verify that "${item.title}" is complete?`);
167	    }
168	  }
169	
170	  return {
171	    ready: !blocking,
172	    questions: Array.from(new Set(questions)),
173	    gaps,
174	    structure,
175	  };
176	}
177

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:48:36.976Z

I'm weighing whether changing the parser to recognize checked-off items would break existing consumers, and decide it's safer to extend it to also match `- [x]`/`- [X]` lines with a new `completed` flag rather than altering current matching behavior.

Rather than risk drift with duplicate parsing logic elsewhere, I want to check the existing test suite for `parsePlan` and readiness to gauge how much this change would actually touch before committing to an approach.

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:48:55.402Z

I'm extending the parser to support checkbox-style list items (pending and completed states) alongside numbered items, updating the completed field and the item-detail collection logic accordingly. Now I'm moving on to planning how the derivation module will resolve an objective to its corresponding plan file.

I need rules for extracting `.md` path tokens from the objective, resolving them relative to the project path, verifying they exist and stay within the project boundary, and capping file size — with a named skip event like `goal.plan.derivation_skipped` for cases like out-of-project references. For ambiguous multiple candidates, I'm deciding to just take the first existing match rather than being overly strict and causing silent failures.

Now I'm thinking through item mapping — each GoalPlanItem needs a deterministic id, and there's already a `createDeterministicGoalPlanItemId` helper, but its signature ties ids to revision, which would cause churn on every refresh. I want stable ids based on goalId, source line, and title instead, so I'm going to check the goal-normalization module to see how that helper works.

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:49:12.834Z

I'm considering adding a stable helper for deriving goal plan item ids without duplicating the hashing logic, weighing whether to reuse the deterministic id function with a fixed revision versus generalizing the shared module to support both cases cleanly.

Now I'm thinking through the `applyDerivedPlan` method on GoalControlService — gating writes by runId, goalId, and revision, skipping cleared statuses, updating the plan JSON fields with a reconciliation transition source, emitting a plan-updated event, and returning a replayed flag when nothing changed. I'm also identifying the pragmatic hook points to wire this into, starting with the path where an agent-created goal session update triggers a plan refresh.

Continuing the hook point list — worker session initialization for existing goals covers both user-set goals reaching a worker and reattach scenarios, and the HTTP PUT route for goals covers UI edits. For refreshing plan progress as turns complete, I want to find where the server marks a direct-mode turn finished, so I'm scanning sync.ts and considering whether plan-stream.ts or runtime-client.ts would be a more robust place to hook when the ACP session reports the turn ending, then searching for where worker status flips to idle.

function resolveSyncedRunState(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect, agent: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord>) {
  if (agent.state === "error") {
    return "failed";
  }

  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({ ...agent, workerStatus: agent.state }) === "awaiting_user") {
    return "awaiting_user";
  }

  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && directLiveAgentHasCompletedTurn(agent)) {
    return "done";
  }

  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && agent.state === "idle" && hasAgentOutput(agent)) {
    return "done";
  }

  if (
    ["stopped", "cancelled", "done", "completed"].includes(agent.state)
    || (agent.state === "idle" && agent.stopReason === "end_turn" && hasAgentOutput(agent))
  ) {
    return "done";
  }

  return "running";
}

async function resolvePersistedRunState(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect, worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
  const status = normalizedStatus(worker.status);

  if (status === "error") {
    return "failed";
  }

  if (status === "lost") {
    return "needs_recovery";
  }

  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({ ...worker, workerStatus: worker.status }) === "awaiting_user") {
    return "awaiting_user";
  }

  if (
    ["stopped", "cancelled", "done", "completed"].includes(status)
    || (status === "idle" && await hasPersistedWorkerOutput(worker))
  ) {
    return "done";
  }

  return "running";
}

async function isEmptyIdlePersistedWorker(worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
  const status = normalizedStatus(worker.status);
  return status === "idle" && !(await hasPersistedWorkerOutput(worker));
}

function isIdleLiveAgentWithoutOutput(agent: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord>) {
  return agent.state === "idle" && !hasAgentOutput(agent);
}

function isAgentBusyRunFailure(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
  return run.status === "failed" && /\bagent is busy\b/i.test(run.lastError ?? "");
}

function isRecoverableImplementationTransientFailure(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
  const lastError = run.lastError ?? "";
  return run.mode === "implementation"
    && run.status === "failed"
    && Boolean(lastError.trim())

```text
  content: string,
  imageAttachments?: Array<{ path: string; mimeType: string }>,
  promptOverride?: string | null,
  expectedTurnGeneration?: number,
) {
  const workerPrompt = promptOverride ?? (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) ? buildDirectWorkerPrompt(content) : content);
  try {
    return await askAgentWithAuthRetry(worker.id, workerPrompt, imageAttachments, expectedTurnGeneration);
  } catch (error) {
    if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error))) {
      const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
      const recreated = await recreateWorkerFromTranscript({
        run,
        worker: currentWorker ?? worker,
        nextUserPrompt: workerPrompt,
        source: "direct-follow-up",
        reason: "provider_session_diagnostic_after_direct_follow_up",
        expectedTurnGeneration,
      });
      await db.update(workers).set({
        status: "working",
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
      await db.update(runs).set({
        status: "running",
        failedAt: null,
        lastError: null,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();

      try {
        return imageAttachments?.length
          ? await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt, imageAttachments, { expectedTurnGeneration })
          : await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt, undefined, { expectedTurnGeneration });
      } catch (retryError) {
        if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError))) {
          throw new Error(userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError)));
        }
        throw retryError;
      }
    }

    if (!isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
      throw error;
    }

    const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
    if (isWorkerCancelled(currentWorker)) {
      throw error;
    }

    const resumedWorker = await resumeMissingDirectWorker(run, currentWorker ?? worker);
    if (!resumedWorker) {
      throw error;
    }

    // resumeMissingDirectWorker just wrote the resumed runtime agent's
    // state ("idle" or "starting") into the DB. The askAgent call on the
    // next line drives the runtime to "working", but no other code path
    // touches the workers row until askAgent resolves — which for a long
    // Claude turn is many minutes. Without re-arming the DB row here the
    // frontend sees the worker as idle for the entire turn and never
    // shows the "Thinking…" indicator.
    await db.update(workers).set({
      status: "working",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
    await db.update(runs).set({
      status: "running",
      failedAt: null,
      lastError: null,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();

    const replayPrompt = resumedWorker.transcriptReplayRequired
      ? await buildTranscriptReplayPrompt({
        runId: run.id,
        workerId: worker.id,
        nextUserPrompt: workerPrompt,
      })
      : null;
    try {
      return imageAttachments?.length
        ? await askAgent(worker.id, replayPrompt ?? workerPrompt, imageAttachments, { expectedTurnGeneration })
        : await askAgent(worker.id, replayPrompt ?? workerPrompt, undefined, { expectedTurnGeneration });
    } catch (retryError) {
      if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError))) {
        throw new Error(userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError)));
      }
      throw retryError;
    }
  }
}

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:49:37.701Z

This is sprawling with too many turn-completion sites scattered across create.ts, send-message.ts, queued-messages.ts, and recovery. I'm reconsidering whether the existing watchdog in sync.ts that sweeps runs might be the cleanest single choke point for periodically refreshing the derived plan, and I want to check how it works and whether goals already touch it there—though there might be a better option in the goalOutboxDispatcher and run_goal_outbox.

Deriving the plan at read time in goalControl.getGoal seems appealing but conflicts with keeping reads side-effect-free, so I'm going to look at sync.ts's entry point and how often it runs.

let bridgeSyncInFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;

/**
 * Watchdog-driven reconciliation between the bridge runtime and persisted
 * worker/run state. Turn completions are normally persisted by the awaiting
 * send-message call, or by the SSE-driven sync that runs while a client is
 * connected — but both die with their in-flight request (server restart,
 * dropped ACP roundtrip, phone backgrounded the tab). A worker then sits at
 * status='working' forever while the bridge agent finished its turn long
 * ago, and the stuck-worker reaper keeps cancelling and re-delivering the
 * same prompt every timeout window. This sweep closes the gap from the 15s
 * watchdog without requiring any connected client.
 */
export function syncConversationSessionsFromBridge(): Promise<void> {
  if (bridgeSyncInFlight) {
    return bridgeSyncInFlight;
  }

  bridgeSyncInFlight = (async () => {
    let rawAgents: unknown[];
    try {
      rawAgents = await listAgents({ retryIndefinitely: false });
    } catch {
      // Bridge down or unreachable — there is no live state to reconcile
      // against; the next sweep will retry.
      return;
    }
    await syncConversationSessions(rawAgents);
  })().finally(() => {
    bridgeSyncInFlight = null;
  });
  return bridgeSyncInFlight;
}

type SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {
  selectedRunId?: string | null;
  refreshPlanningArtifacts?: boolean;
};

let liveSyncQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

export function __resetSyncConversationSessionsQueueForTests() {
  liveSyncQueue = Promise.resolve();
}

export async function syncConversationSessions(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {
  const runSync = () => syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents, options);
  const nextSync = liveSyncQueue.then(runSync, runSync);
  liveSyncQueue = nextSync.catch(() => undefined);
  return nextSync;
}

async function syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {

    ? await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, selectedRunId))
    : await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents);

    incident.kind === "quota_exhausted"

          status: "quota_waiting",

    const staleImplementationConnectionFailure = isRecoverableImplementationConnectionFailure(run);

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:50:02.403Z

I found the direct-mode turn-boundary check that triggers when a run reaches awaiting_user or done — a good hook for refreshing the derived goal plan. Now I'm settling the design, starting with the parser module that needs to match checkbox syntax variants like `- [ ]`, `- [x]`, and `- [X]`.

For the shared module, I'll extract a hashing helper and a function that constructs deterministic IDs for derived goal-plan items based on goal, source, line, and title. The core piece is a new module handling goal-plan derivation from objectives — it needs to locate a plan artifact path referenced in the objective text, derive the plan from it, and refresh it when triggered. I'm working through the logic: skip if there's no visible goal, only proceed if the plan is currently server-derivable (either empty with no source, or previously derived by us via a matching URI), otherwise skip because the provider owns the plan — then scan the objective text for markdown file references to resolve the artifact path.

I'm validating the resolved path exists as a file within the project directory and under the max length limit, then reading and parsing it into checklist items — skipping if none are found, capping the count and truncating titles, then applying the derived plan through goal control and emitting a derivation event (or skip event on early returns). I still need to wire in `applyDerivedPlan` on the goal-control side, register new event kinds for derivation success/skip/failure, hook this into goal creation, worker session initialization, the PUT route for goals, and turn boundaries in sync, then add corresponding tests — no new i18n strings are needed since this is provider-facing.

I'm also considering whether the derived plan should reference the source file via a `file://` URI so the plan card can offer an "open plan" action, converting the absolute path with `pathToFileURL`, and double-checking that the existing locale key and event naming conventions support this.

1	import { dbClient } from "@/server/db";
2	import { requireApiSession } from "@/server/auth/guards";
3	import { emitNamedEvent, type SurfacedErrorCode } from "@/server/events/named-events";
4	import { goalControl } from "@/server/runs/goal-control";
5	import { goalControlDispatchCoordinator, recoveryAction } from "@/server/runs/goal-control-dispatch";
6	import { goalOutboxDispatcher } from "@/server/runs/goal-outbox";
7	import { goalRateLimitManager } from "@/server/runs/goal-rate-limit";
8	import { redactGoalErrorMessage } from "@/server/runs/goal-errors";
9	import { attachGoalMutationToActiveWorker } from "@/server/runs/goal-worker-lease";
10	import {
11	  GOAL_ACTIONS,
12	  type GoalAction,
13	  type GoalMutationAction,
14	  type GoalMutationResult,
15	} from "@/shared/goal-plan";
16	import type { OmniHttpHandler } from "@/runtime/http/registry";
17	
18	const MAX_GOAL_REQUEST_BYTES = 64 * 1024;
19	const MAX_IDENTIFIER_LENGTH = 128;
20	
21	type MutationBody = {
22	  goalId: string;
23	  expectedRevision: number;
24	  operationId: string;
25	  objective?: string;
26	  action?: GoalAction;
27	};
28	
29	function errorResponse(status: number, code: string, message: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
30	  const goal = extra.goal;
31	  return Response.json({
32	    error: {
33	      code,
34	      message,
35	      ...(goal && typeof goal === "object" ? { details: { goal } } : {}),
36	    },
37	    ...extra,
38	  }, { status });
39	}
40	
41	function nonEmptyIdentifier(value: unknown) {
42	  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 && value.length <= MAX_IDENTIFIER_LENGTH;
43	}
44	
45	function parseMutationBody(value: unknown, method: string): MutationBody | null {
46	  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return null;
47	  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;
48	  if (
49	    !nonEmptyIdentifier(candidate.goalId)
50	    || !nonEmptyIdentifier(candidate.operationId)
51	    || !Number.isSafeInteger(candidate.expectedRevision)
52	    || Number(candidate.expectedRevision) < 0
53	  ) {
54	    return null;
55	  }
56	  if (method === "PUT" && typeof candidate.objective !== "string") return null;
57	  if (method === "POST" && !GOAL_ACTIONS.includes(candidate.action as GoalAction)) return null;
58	  return {
59	    goalId: String(candidate.goalId).trim(),
60	    operationId: String(candidate.operationId).trim(),
61	    expectedRevision: Number(candidate.expectedRevision),
62	    ...(method === "PUT" ? { objective: candidate.objective as string } : {}),
63	    ...(method === "POST" ? { action: candidate.action as GoalAction } : {}),
64	  };
65	}
66	
67	async function readJsonBody(request: Request) {
68	  const declaredLength = Number(request.headers.get("content-length") ?? 0);
69	  if (Number.isFinite(declaredLength) && declaredLength > MAX_GOAL_REQUEST_BYTES) {
70	    return { ok: false as const, response: errorResponse(413, "goal.payload_too_large", "The goal request is too large.") };
71	  }
72	  if (!request.headers.get("content-type")?.toLowerCase().includes("application/json")) {
73	    return { ok: false as const, response: errorResponse(415, "goal.payload.invalid", "The goal request must be JSON.") };
74	  }
75	  const text = await request.text();
76	  if (new TextEncoder().encode(text).byteLength > MAX_GOAL_REQUEST_BYTES) {
77	    return { ok: false as const, response: errorResponse(413, "goal.payload_too_large", "The goal request is too large.") };
78	  }
79	  try {
80	    return { ok: true as const, value: JSON.parse(text) as unknown };
81	  } catch {
82	    return { ok: false as const, response: errorResponse(400, "goal.payload.invalid", "The goal request is malformed.") };
83	  }
84	}
85	
86	async function runExists(runId: string) {
87	  const result = await dbClient.execute({ sql: "SELECT id FROM runs WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1", args: [runId] });
88	  return Boolean(result.rows[0]);
89	}
90	
91	function mutationFailureResponse(
92	  result: Extract<GoalMutationResult, { ok: false }>,
93	  details: { runId: string; goalId: string; operationId: string; action: GoalMutationAction },
94	) {
95	  const mapping: Record<typeof result.code, { status: number; publicCode: SurfacedErrorCode }> = {
96	    invalid_objective: { status: 400, publicCode: "goal.objective.invalid" },
97	    not_found: { status: 404, publicCode: "goal.persistence.failed" },
98	    revision_conflict: { status: 409, publicCode: "goal.revision_conflict" },
99	    operation_conflict: { status: 409, publicCode: "goal.revision_conflict" },
100	    invalid_transition: { status: 409, publicCode: "goal.transition.invalid" },
101	    stale_lease: { status: 409, publicCode: "goal.lease.stale" },
102	    unsupported_action: { status: 422, publicCode: "goal.action.unsupported" },
103	    provider_error: { status: 502, publicCode: "goal.acp.transport_failed" },
104	    persistence_error: { status: 500, publicCode: "goal.persistence.failed" },
105	  };
106	  const mapped = mapping[result.code];
107	  if (details.action === "set" || details.action === "edit") {
108	    emitNamedEvent({
109	      kind: "goal.set.refused",
110	      runId: details.runId,
111	      goalId: details.goalId,
112	      operationId: details.operationId,
113	      reason: result.code,
114	    });
115	  } else {
116	    emitNamedEvent({
117	      kind: "goal.action.refused",
118	      runId: details.runId,
119	      goalId: details.goalId,
120	      operationId: details.operationId,
121	      action: details.action,
122	      reason: result.code,
123	    });
124	  }
125	  emitNamedEvent({
126	    kind: "error.surfaced",
127	    code: mapped.publicCode,
128	    message: result.message,
129	    surface: "banner",
130	    runId: details.runId,
131	  });
132	  return Response.json({
133	    error: { code: mapped.publicCode, message: result.message, details: { goal: result.snapshot } },
134	    goal: result.snapshot,
135	  }, { status: mapped.status });
136	}
137	
138	async function dispatchAndPublish(
139	  result: Extract<GoalMutationResult, { ok: true }>,
140	  action: GoalMutationAction,
141	  operationId: string,
142	) {
143	  const control = await goalControlDispatchCoordinator.dispatch(
144	    result.snapshot,
145	    result.replayed ? recoveryAction(result.snapshot) : action,
146	  );
147	  if (control.kind === "deferred") {
148	    emitNamedEvent({
149	      kind: "goal.reconciliation.refused",
150	      runId: control.snapshot.runId,
151	      goalId: control.snapshot.goalId,
152	      workerId: control.snapshot.workerId,
153	      reason: control.reason,
154	    });
155	  }
156	  if (control.kind !== "unsupported" && control.kind !== "superseded" && action !== "set" && action !== "edit") {
157	      emitNamedEvent({
158	        kind: "goal.action.completed",
159	        runId: control.snapshot.runId,
160	        goalId: control.snapshot.goalId,
161	        operationId,
162	        action,
163	        revision: control.snapshot.revision,
164	      });
165	  }
166	  return control;
167	}
168	
169	export const handleGoalRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request, context) => {
170	  const runId = context.params?.id?.trim();
171	  if (!runId) return errorResponse(400, "goal.payload.invalid", "A run id is required.");
172	
173	  const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
174	    action: request.method === "GET" ? "Read goal" : "Change goal",
175	    source: "Goal",
176	    enforceSameOrigin: request.method !== "GET",
177	  });
178	  if (auth.response) return auth.response;
179	
180	  let exists: boolean;
181	  try {
182	    exists = await runExists(runId);
183	  } catch (error) {
184	    const message = redactGoalErrorMessage(error);
185	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.persistence.failed", message, surface: "banner", runId });
186	    return errorResponse(500, "goal.persistence.failed", "The goal state could not be loaded.");
187	  }
188	  if (!exists) {
189	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.not_found", message: "The run does not exist.", surface: "banner", runId });
190	    return errorResponse(404, "goal.not_found", "The run does not exist.");
191	  }
192	  if (request.method === "GET") {
193	    return Response.json({ goal: await goalControl.getGoal(runId) });
194	  }
195	  if (request.method !== "PUT" && request.method !== "POST") {
196	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.payload.invalid", message: "The goal request method is not supported.", surface: "banner", runId });
197	    return errorResponse(405, "goal.method_not_allowed", "The goal request method is not supported.");
198	  }
199	
200	  const parsedBody = await readJsonBody(request);
201	  if (!parsedBody.ok) {
202	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.payload.invalid", message: "The goal request payload was rejected.", surface: "banner", runId });
203	    return parsedBody.response;
204	  }
205	  const body = parseMutationBody(parsedBody.value, request.method);
206	  if (!body) {
207	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.payload.invalid", message: "The goal request is invalid.", surface: "banner", runId });
208	    return errorResponse(400, "goal.payload.invalid", "The goal request is invalid.");
209	  }
210	  const principalId = auth.session?.id ?? `local:${context.surface}`;
211	  const rateLimit = goalRateLimitManager.check({ principalId, runId, endpoint: request.method });
212	  if (!rateLimit.allowed) {
213	    const message = "Too many goal changes were requested. Try again shortly.";
214	    emitNamedEvent(request.method === "PUT"
215	      ? { kind: "goal.set.refused", runId, goalId: body.goalId, operationId: body.operationId, reason: "rate_limited" }
216	      : { kind: "goal.action.refused", runId, goalId: body.goalId, operationId: body.operationId, action: body.action!, reason: "rate_limited" });
217	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.rate_limited", message, surface: "banner", runId });
218	    return Response.json({ error: { code: "goal.rate_limited", message } }, {
219	      status: 429,
220	      headers: { "retry-after": String(Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rateLimit.retryAfterMs / 1_000))) },
221	    });
222	  }
223	  const currentGoal = request.method === "PUT" ? await goalControl.getGoal(runId) : null;
224	  const action: GoalMutationAction = request.method === "PUT"
225	    ? (!currentGoal || (currentGoal.status === "cleared" && currentGoal.goalId !== body.goalId) ? "set" : "edit")
226	    : body.action!;
227	
228	  if (request.method === "PUT") {
229	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "goal.set.started", runId, goalId: body.goalId, operationId: body.operationId });
230	  } else {
231	    emitNamedEvent({
232	      kind: "goal.action.started",
233	      runId,
234	      goalId: body.goalId,
235	      operationId: body.operationId,
236	      action: body.action!,
237	    });
238	  }
239	
240	  let result: GoalMutationResult;
241	  try {
242	    result = request.method === "PUT"
243	      ? await goalControl.putGoal({
244	          runId,
245	          goalId: body.goalId,
246	          expectedRevision: body.expectedRevision,
247	          operationId: body.operationId,
248	          objective: body.objective!,
249	          principalId,
250	          endpoint: "goal.put",
251	        })
252	      : await goalControl.actionGoal({
253	          runId,
254	          goalId: body.goalId,
255	          expectedRevision: body.expectedRevision,
256	          operationId: body.operationId,

266	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "error.surfaced", code: "goal.persistence.failed", message, surface: "banner", runId });

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:50:32.148Z

This route seems too risky — inserting derivation mid-flow could break the finalizeOperation/dispatch fencing since applyDerivedPlan bumps revision, and I need to find a safe moment to bump it. The safest spot looks like right after handleAcpGoalSessionUpdateForWorker completes and returns accepted, since everything's already settled by then.

I'm checking initializeWorkerGoalSession too — its result is unused by the caller, so bumping revision there after the dispatch coordinator settles should be safe, and the same goes for the sync.ts direct-run boundary since there's no in-flight fence there. The HTTP route is trickier: bumping before dispatchAndPublish invalidates the revision used for dispatch, and bumping after leaves the returned snapshot stale by one revision, which could cause the frontend to hit a revision_conflict on its next action even though the SSE event would eventually deliver the correct snapshot. Given that risk, the cleanest choice is to skip doing this in the HTTP route entirely and rely on initializeWorkerGoalSession and sync.ts, which run at session init and turn boundaries respectively.

Idle conversations are still covered because the sweep runs every 15 seconds and my hook fires whenever a direct-run session is in the done or awaiting_user state, so a goal set on an idle conversation gets picked up within that window. That does mean every sweep triggers a DB read, path resolution, file stat, and a full read-and-parse-and-compare of the JSON for each active goal-bearing direct run, which is wasteful even though it's a no-op when nothing's changed — I should add some kind of mtime check to avoid the redundant work.

I'm considering caching run-to-file-metadata mappings to skip re-reads, but that risks missing cases where the goal's plan gets cleared or replaced even though the file itself hasn't changed. Since that's a rare edge case, the simplest and safest approach is to skip caching altogether and just do the stat plus conditional read every time.

I still need to pull projectPath from the runs table for the derivation module, and I'm deciding to keep the plan-derivation logic mode-agnostic since it's really about the goal and objective referencing a plan file rather than being tied to any particular run mode. For safety, I'll require the resolved plan file path to live inside the run's project directory rather than allowing arbitrary paths, and if projectPath is missing for a run, I'll just skip derivation with a clear reason code. Time to start writing the parser changes.

  "old_string": "export interface ParsedPlanItem {\n  id: string;\n  phase: string | null;\n  title: string;\n  sourceLine: number;\n  details?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface ParsedPlan {\n  markdown: string;\n  items: ParsedPlanItem[];\n}\n\nexport function parsePlan(markdown: string): ParsedPlan {\n  const lines = markdown.split(\"\\n\");\n  const items: ParsedPlanItem[] = [];\n  let currentPhase: string | null = null;\n\n  for (const [index, line] of lines.entries()) {\n    const phaseMatch = line.match(/^##\\s+(.+)$/);\n    if (phaseMatch) {\n      currentPhase = phaseMatch[1].trim();\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const itemMatch = line.match(/^(?:- \\[ \\]|\\d+\\.)\\s+(.+)$/);\n    if (itemMatch) {\n      items.push({\n        id: `item-${index + 1}`,\n        phase: currentPhase,\n        title: itemMatch[1].trim(),\n        sourceLine: index + 1,\n        details: collectItemDetails(lines, index),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { markdown, items };\n}\n\nfunction collectItemDetails(lines: string[], itemIndex: number) {\n  const details: string[] = [];\n\n  for (let index = itemIndex + 1; index < lines.length; index += 1) {\n    const line = lines[index];\n    if (/^#{1,6}\\s+(.+)$/.test(line) || /^(?:- \\[ \\]|\\d+\\.)\\s+(.+)$/.test(line)) {\n      break;\n    }\n    if (line.trim().length > 0) {\n      details.push(line.trim());\n    }\n  }\n\n  return details.join(\"\\n\");\n}",
  "new_string": "export interface ParsedPlanItem {\n  id: string;\n  phase: string | null;\n  title: string;\n  sourceLine: number;\n  /** True when the source line is a ticked checkbox (`- [x]`). */\n  completed: boolean;\n  details?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface ParsedPlan {\n  markdown: string;\n  items: ParsedPlanItem[];\n}\n\nconst ITEM_PATTERN = /^(?:- \\[( |x|X)\\]|\\d+\\.)\\s+(.+)$/;\n\nexport function parsePlan(markdown: string): ParsedPlan {\n  const lines = markdown.split(\"\\n\");\n  const items: ParsedPlanItem[] = [];\n  let currentPhase: string | null = null;\n\n  for (const [index, line] of lines.entries()) {\n    const phaseMatch = line.match(/^##\\s+(.+)$/);\n    if (phaseMatch) {\n      currentPhase = phaseMatch[1].trim();\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const itemMatch = line.match(ITEM_PATTERN);\n    if (itemMatch) {\n      items.push({\n        id: `item-${index + 1}`,\n        phase: currentPhase,\n        title: itemMatch[2].trim(),\n        sourceLine: index + 1,\n        completed: itemMatch[1] === \"x\" || itemMatch[1] === \"X\",\n        details: collectItemDetails(lines, index),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { markdown, items };\n}\n\nfunction collectItemDetails(lines: string[], itemIndex: number) {\n  const details: string[] = [];\n\n  for (let index = itemIndex + 1; index < lines.length; index += 1) {\n    const line = lines[index];\n    if (/^#{1,6}\\s+(.+)$/.test(line) || ITEM_PATTERN.test(line)) {\n      break;\n    }\n    if (line.trim().length > 0) {\n      details.push(line.trim());\n    }\n  }\n\n  return details.join(\"\\n\");\n}"
}

210	    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
211	  }
212	  return (hash >>> 0).toString(36);
213	}
214	
215	/**
216	 * Id for a plan item the server derived from a plan file rather than from a
217	 * provider payload. Unlike the provider id it is deliberately independent of
218	 * the goal revision: the same checklist line keeps the same id across every
219	 * re-derivation, so rendered rows stay stable while the file is edited and the
220	 * goal revision advances.
221	 */
222	export function createDerivedGoalPlanItemId(input: {
223	  goalId: string;
224	  source: string;
225	  sourceLine: number;
226	  title: string;
227	}) {
228	  return `goal-item-${goalPlanItemToken(
229	    `${input.goalId} ${input.source} ${input.sourceLine} ${input.title}`,
230	  )}`;
231	}
232	
233	export function createDeterministicGoalPlanItemId(input: {
234	  goalId: string;
235	  revision: number;
236	  title: string;
237	  phase?: string | null;
238	}) {
239	  const value = `${input.goalId}\u0000${input.revision}\u0000${input.phase ?? ""}\u0000${input.title}`;
240	  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
241	  for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
242	    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(index);
243	    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
244	  }
245	  return `goal-item-${(hash >>> 0).toString(36)}`;
246	}
247

kind: \"failed\"; reason: string };\n\nconst MARKDOWN_REFERENCE_PATTERN = /(?:[A-Za-z]:)?[\\w./\\\\~-]*\\.md\\b/g;\n\nfunction skip(runId: string, goalId: string | null, reason: GoalPlanDerivationSkipReason): GoalPlanDerivationResult {\n  emitNamedEvent({ kind: \"goal.plan.derivation_skipped\", runId, goalId, reason });\n  return { kind: \"skipped\", reason };\n}\n\nfunction isInside(parent: string, candidate: string) {\n  const relative = path.relative(parent, candidate);\n  return relative === \"\" || (!relative.startsWith(\"..\") && !path.isAbsolute(relative));\n}\n\n/**\n * Candidate markdown paths named by a goal objective, in the order they appear.\n * Quoting, trailing punctuation and backticks are stripped; resolution objective.matchAll(MARKDOWN_REFERENCE_PATTERN)) {\n    const raw = match[0].replace(/^[\"'`(<]+/, \"\").replace(/[\"'`)>,.]+$/, \"\");\n    if (raw && !references.includes(raw)) references.push(raw);\n  }\n  return references;\n}\n\nfunction resolvePlanReference(objective: string, projectPath: string) {\n  for (const reference of extractPlanReferencesFromObjective(objective)) {\n    const absolute = path.resolve(projectPath, reference.replace(/\\\\/g, path.sep));\n    if (!isInside(projectPath, absolute)) return { kind: \"outside\" as const };\n    let stats: fs.Stats;\n    try {\n      stats = fs.statSync(absolute);\n    } catch {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!stats.isFile()) continue;\n    return { kind: \"found\" as const, absolute, size: stats.size };\n  }\n  return { kind: \"missing\" as const };\n}\n\nfunction buildDerivedPlanItems(goalId: string, source: string, markdown: string): GoalPlanItem[] {\n  return parsePlan(markdown).items.slice(0, GOAL_PLAN_MAX_ITEMS).map((item, order) => {\n    const title = Array.from(item.title).slice(0, GOAL_PLAN_ITEM_MAX_LENGTH).join(\"\");\n    return {\n      id: createDerivedGoalPlanItemId({ goalId, source, sourceLine: item.sourceLine, title }),\n      title,\n      phase: item.phase,\n      status: item.completed ? \"completed\" : \"pending\",\n      order,\n      providerId: null,\n    } satisfies GoalPlanItem;\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * A goal plan is derivable while nothing provider-owned occupies the slot:\n * either the plan is empty, or the goal already carries a plan this same\n * derivation produced from the same file.\n */\nfunction isDerivable(goal: GoalSnapshot, derivedUri: string | null) {\n  if (goal.plan.length === 0 && goal.planSource.kind === \"none\") return true;\n  return goal.planSource.kind === \"uri\"\n    && goal.planSource.uri === derivedUri\n    && goal.plan.every((item) => item.providerId === null);\n}\n\nexport async function refreshDerivedGoalPlan(\n  runId: string,\n  trigger: \"goal_created\" | \"session_initialized\" | \"turn_settled\",\n): Promise<GoalPlanDerivationResult> {\n  try {\n    const goal = await goalControl.getGoal(runId);\n    if (!goal) return skip(runId, null, \"goal_absent\");\n    if (!goal.visible || goal.status === \"cleared\") return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"goal_not_visible\");\n\n    const run = await db.select({ projectPath: runs.projectPath }).from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();\n    if (!run) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"run_missing\");\n    const projectPath = run.projectPath?.trim();\n    if (!projectPath) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"project_path_unknown\");\n\n    const reference = resolvePlanReference(goal.objective, path.resolve(projectPath));\n    if (reference.kind === \"outside\") return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"reference_outside_project\");\n    if (reference.kind === \"missing\") {\n      return skip(\n        runId,\n        goal.goalId,\n        extractPlanReferencesFromObjective(goal.objective).length > 0 ? \"reference_missing\" : \"no_plan_reference\",\n      );\n    }\n    if (reference.size > GOAL_PLAN_MARKDOWN_MAX_LENGTH) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"reference_too_large\");\n\n    const uri = pathToFileURL(reference.absolute).toString();\n    if (!isDerivable(goal, uri)) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"provider_owns_plan\");\n\n    const plan = buildDerivedPlanItems(goal.goalId, uri, fs.readFileSync(reference.absolute, \"utf8\"));\n    if (plan.length === 0) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"no_checklist_items\");\n\n    const applied = await goalControl.applyDerivedPlan({\n      runId,\n      goalId: goal.goalId,\n      expectedRevision: goal.revision,\n      plan,\n      planSource: { kind: \"uri\", uri },\n    });\n    if (!applied.ok) {\n      emitNamedEvent({ kind: \"goal.plan.derivation_refused\", runId, goalId: goal.goalId, reason: applied.code });\n      return { kind: \"refused\", reason: applied.code };\n    }\n    if (applied.replayed) return skip(runId, goal.goalId, \"unchanged\");\n\n    await goalOutboxDispatcher.drainPending();\n    emitNamedEvent({\n      kind: \"goal.plan.derived\",\n      runId,\n      goalId: goal.goalId,\n      source: uri,\n      itemCount: plan.length,\n      completedCount: plan.filter((item) => item.status === \"completed\").length,\n      revision: applied.snapshot.revision,\n      trigger,\n    });\n    return { kind: \"derived\", itemCount: plan.length, source: uri, revision: applied.snapshot.revision };\n  } catch (error) {\n    const reason = redactGoalErrorMessage(error);\n    emitNamedEvent({ kind: \"goal.plan.derivation_failed\", runId, reason });\n    emitNamedEvent({\n      kind: \"error.surfaced\",\n      code: \"goal.plan.derivation_failed\",\n      message: `The plan referenced by this goal could not be read: ${reason}`,\n      surface: \"log\",\n      runId,\n    });\n    return { kind: \"failed\", reason };\n  }\n}\n"

  "old_string": "  async isControlSettled(snapshot: GoalSnapshot) {",
  "new_string": "  /**\n   * Write a plan the server derived from a plan file. Unlike a provider update\n   * this carries no worker lease: derivation is a property of the objective and\n   * the file it names, not of whichever agent happens to hold the session, so\n   * it stays valid across worker restarts and reattaches. The revision fence\n   * still applies, so a concurrent provider update wins and the derivation is\n   * simply refused.\n   */\n  async applyDerivedPlan(input: DerivedGoalPlanRequest): Promise<GoalMutationResult> {\n    return retryGoalBusy(() => this.applyDerivedPlanOnce(input));\n  }\n\n  private async applyDerivedPlanOnce(input: DerivedGoalPlanRequest): Promise<GoalMutationResult> {\n    const transaction = await this.client.transaction(\"write\");\n    try {\n      const current = await selectGoal(transaction, input.runId);\n      if (!current) {\n        await transaction.commit();\n        return goalFailure(\"not_found\", \"The goal does not exist.\", null);\n      }\n      if (current.status === \"cleared\") {\n        await transaction.commit();\n        return goalFailure(\"invalid_transition\", \"A cleared goal cannot be updated.\", current);\n      }\n      const conflict = this.validateMutationFence(current, input);\n      if (conflict) {\n        await transaction.commit();\n        return conflict;\n      }\n      if (\n        JSON.stringify(input.plan) === JSON.stringify(current.plan)\n        && JSON.stringify(input.planSource) === JSON.stringify(current.planSource)\n      ) {\n        await transaction.commit();\n        return { ok: true, snapshot: current, replayed: true };\n      }\n      const now = this.now().getTime();\n      const revision = current.revision + 1;\n      await transaction.execute({\n        sql: `UPDATE run_goals SET plan_json = ?, plan_source_json = ?, revision = ?,\n                transition_source = 'reconciliation', updated_at = ?\n              WHERE run_id = ? AND goal_id = ? AND revision = ? AND status != 'cleared'`,\n        args: [\n          JSON.stringify(input.plan),\n          JSON.stringify(input.planSource),\n          revision,\n          now,\n          input.runId,\n          input.goalId,\n          input.expectedRevision,\n        ],\n      });\n      const snapshot = await selectGoal(transaction, input.runId);\n      if (!snapshot || snapshot.revision !== revision) {\n        await transaction.rollback();\n        return goalFailure(\"revision_conflict\", \"The goal changed before the derived plan was committed.\", snapshot);\n      }\n      await enqueueGoalEvent(transaction, snapshot, \"goal.plan.updated\", now, this.randomId);\n      await transaction.commit();\n      return { ok: true, snapshot, replayed: false };\n    } catch (error) {\n      await transaction.rollback();\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  async isControlSettled(snapshot: GoalSnapshot) {"
}

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T19:52:24.539Z

I'm double-checking that re-reading the goal after the bump gives the correct revision, so the provider update will apply with the right expected revision — and confirming the snapshot shape from getGoal matches what's already attached, so that's consistent.

For initializeWorkerGoalSession, I need to insert the derivation step before the final reconciliation.completed event fires. But there's a risk: settledSnapshot gets returned to callers and used in tests, so if I derive afterward it'll be stale — I should refresh settledSnapshot right after derivation and before emitting that final event.

I'm also tracing whether bumping the revision afterward breaks isControlSettled, since markControlApplied writes a marker on the row rather than per-revision, and I need to verify the matching logic still holds against the new revision.

The cleanest fix is to move the derivation to the very start of initializeWorkerGoalSession, right after the visibility check but before attachLease, then re-read the current goal state so attachLease and dispatch use the post-derivation revision consistently.

47	function mutate(path: string, method: "PUT" | "POST", body: unknown) {
48	  return client.fetch(path, {
49	    method,
50	    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
51	    body: JSON.stringify(body),
52	  });

55	describe("lifecycle — durable goal control plane", () => {
56	  it("sets, publishes, bootstraps, and rejects a stale edit through HTTP/SSE", async () => {
57	    const { runId } = await seedDirectRun();
58	    await client.bootstrapSnapshot(runId);
59	    await client.subscribe({ runId });
60	
61	    const created = await mutate(`/api/runs/${runId}/goal`, "PUT", {

63	      expectedRevision: 0,
64	      operationId: "op-set",

+   * Write a plan the server derived from a plan file. Unlike a provider update
+   * this carries no worker lease: derivation is a property of the objective and
+   * the file it names, not of whichever agent happens to hold the session, so
+   * it stays valid across worker restarts and reattaches. The revision fence
+   * still applies, so a concurrent provider update wins and the derivation is
+   * simply refused.
+   */

+    return retryGoalBusy(() => this.applyDerivedPlanOnce(input));
+  }
+
+  private async applyDerivedPlanOnce(input: DerivedGoalPlanRequest): Promise<GoalMutationResult> {
+    const transaction = await this.client.transaction("write");
+    try {
+      const current = await selectGoal(transaction, input.runId);
+      if (!current) {
+        await transaction.commit();
+        return goalFailure("not_found", "The goal does not exist.", null);
+      }
+      if (current.status === "cleared") {
+        await transaction.commit();
+        return goalFailure("invalid_transition", "A cleared goal cannot be updated.", current);
+      }
+      const conflict = this.validateMutationFence(current, input);
+      if (conflict) {
+        await transaction.commit();
+        return conflict;
+      }
+      if (
+        JSON.stringify(input.plan) === JSON.stringify(current.plan)
+        && JSON.stringify(input.planSource) === JSON.stringify(current.planSource)

+        return { ok: true, snapshot: current, replayed: true };
+      }
+      const now = this.now().getTime();
+      const revision = current.revision + 1;

+        sql: `UPDATE run_goals SET plan_json = ?, plan_source_json = ?, revision = ?,
+                transition_source = 'reconciliation', updated_at = ?
+              WHERE run_id = ? AND goal_id = ? AND revision = ? AND status != 'cleared'`,

+          input.runId,
+          input.goalId,
+          input.expectedRevision,
+        ],
+      });
+      const snapshot = await selectGoal(transaction, input.runId);

+    } catch (error) {
+      await transaction.rollback();
+      throw error;
+    }

}

function goalPlanItemToken(value: string) {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(36);
}

/**
 * Id for a plan item the server derived from a plan file rather than from a
 * provider payload. Unlike the provider id it is deliberately independent of
 * the goal revision: the same checklist line keeps the same id across every
 * re-derivation, so rendered rows stay stable while the file is edited and the
 * goal revision advances.
 */

}

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-05T20:00:49.099Z

I've confirmed the diagnosis: the run's plan data is empty in both the plans table and run_goals, and the worker stream never emitted a single ACP plan update despite announcing its goal — Codex simply never sent a plan, which is why the goal card shows "No plan" in the UI.

The fix is to derive the plan from the markdown file the objective references, though I should flag that this particular plan file has 56 unchecked boxes, so the resulting conversation would honestly show 0/56 progress rather than completion. I also need to note how an existing conversation would pick up the fix — through session reinit or its next settled turn — and I should summarize verification: unit tests, the lifecycle scenario, and the full suite matching baseline (15 pre-existing failures, 2 flakes confirmed passing on rerun), plus a clean typecheck aside from one pre-existing error, and a successful web build. I'll keep the write-up tight.

<!-- codex-event:{"kind":"final","timestamp":"2026-09-05T20:00:59.036Z","phase":null} -->
## Claude · 2026-09-05T20:00:59.036Z

Diagnosed and fixed. The goal card was telling the truth — nothing had ever put a plan there.

**What the data showed for `05d9fad55def`:**
- `runs.plan_id` → a `plans` row pointing at `vibes/ad-hoc/2026-09-05T15-26-59…md`, the scratch file that just records the original command. Zero `plan_items`.
- `run_goals` had `plan_json = []` and `plan_source_json = {"kind":"none"}`. The goal itself was announced by the agent (`goal.put` from principal `acp:05d9fad55def-worker-1`) with the objective "Fully implement …/2026-09-05-caption-font-sizing-contract.md".
- The worker stream has 12,865 entries and not one ACP `plan` / `plan_update` / `update_plan` among them. Codex ran the whole two-hour implementation without ever emitting a plan.

`GoalPlanCard` renders `PlanProgress` from `goal.plan`, so an empty array renders "No plan items yet" — for a run whose entire objective was a plan file sitting on disk with 56 checklist items.

**The fix:** when a goal's objective names a markdown plan file inside the run's project, the server derives the checklist from that file.

- `src/server/runs/goal-plan-derivation.ts` — resolves the reference (absolute or project-relative), refuses paths outside the project, parses it, and writes the items with `planSource: {kind:"uri", uri:"file://…"}`, which also lights up the existing "Open full plan" button.
- `src/server/runs/goal-control.ts` — `applyDerivedPlan`, a lease-free but revision-fenced write that enqueues `goal.plan.updated` like any other plan change. A real provider plan always wins: derivation only runs while the plan slot is empty or holds its own earlier output.
- `src/server/plans/parser.ts` — `- [x]` lines now parse, carrying a `completed` flag, so ticked boxes show as completed items.
- Hooks: goal creation over ACP, worker session init, and settled direct-mode turns (so progress refreshes as the agent ticks boxes). Every path emits a typed named event, including each skip reason.

**One thing to flag:** that plan file has 56 boxes and the agent ticked none of them, so this conversation will read 0/56 rather than complete. The checklist appears; the progress reflects what the agent actually wrote to the file. The existing conversation picks up its plan when you reopen it or on its next settled turn — no backfill needed.

**Verification:** 7 new unit tests plus a lifecycle scenario driving the exact Codex shape (goal metadata, no plan) through HTTP/SSE. Full suite matches baseline — the same 15 pre-existing failures, and the two extras in my first run (`events-route` scoping, `runner/process` bind) pass on re-run. Typecheck clean apart from the pre-existing `conversation-transcript-retention` error. `pnpm build:interface:web` passes.
